<section id=abstract class=introductory>
<h2>
Abstract
</h2>
<p>
This
specification
extends
the
High
Resolution
Time
specification
[
<cite>
<a class=bibref data-link-type=biblio href=#bib-hr-time-3 title="High Resolution Time">
HR-TIME-3
</a>
</cite>
]
by
providing
methods
to
store
and
retrieve
high
resolution
performance
metric
data.
</p>
</section>
<section id=sotd class=introductory>
<h2>
Status
of
This
Document
</h2>
<details class=annoying-warning open>
<summary>
This
is
a
preview
</summary>
<p>
Do
not
attempt
to
implement
this
version
of
the
specification.
Do
not
reference
this
version
as
authoritative
in
any
way.
Instead,
see
<a href="https://w3c.github.io/performance-timeline/">
https://w3c.github.io/performance-timeline/
</a>
for
the
Editor's
draft.
</p>
</details>
<p>
<em>
This
section
describes
the
status
of
this
document
at
the
time
of
its
publication.
A
list
of
current
<abbr title="World Wide Web Consortium">
W3C
</abbr>
publications
and
the
latest
revision
of
this
technical
report
can
be
found
in
the
<a href="https://www.w3.org/TR/">
<abbr title="World Wide Web Consortium">
W3C
</abbr>
technical
reports
index
</a>
at
https://www.w3.org/TR/.
</em>
</p>
<p>
<ins class="diff-new">This
</ins>
Performance
Timeline
<del class="diff-old">Level
2
</del>
<ins class="diff-chg">specification
</ins>
replaces
the
first
version
of
[
<cite>
<a class=bibref data-link-type=biblio href=#bib-performance-timeline title="Performance Timeline">
PERFORMANCE-TIMELINE
</a>
</cite>
]
and
includes:
</p>
<ul>
<li>
Extends
the
base
definition
of
the
<a data-link-type=idl data-lt=Performance data-type=interface href="https://www.w3.org/TR/hr-time-3/#dom-performance">
<code>
Performance
</code>
</a>
interface
defined
by
[
<cite>
<a class=bibref data-link-type=biblio href=#bib-hr-time-3 title="High Resolution Time">
HR-TIME-3
</a>
</cite>
];
</li>
<li>
Exposes
<a data-link-type=idl data-lt=PerformanceEntry href=#dom-performanceentry class=internalDFN id=ref-for-dom-performanceentry-1>
<code>
PerformanceEntry
</code>
</a>
in
Web
Workers
[
<cite>
<a class=bibref data-link-type=biblio href=#bib-workers title="Web Workers">
WORKERS
</a>
</cite>
];
</li>
<li>
Adds
support
for
<a data-link-type=idl data-lt=PerformanceObserver href=#dom-performanceobserver class=internalDFN id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserver-1>
<code>
PerformanceObserver
</code>
</a>.
</li>
</ul>
<p>
This
document
was
published
by
the
<a href="https://www.w3.org/groups/wg/webperf">
Web
Performance
Working
Group
</a>
as
an
Editor's
Draft.
<p>
Publication
as
an
Editor's
Draft
does
not
imply
endorsement
by
<abbr title="World Wide Web Consortium">
W3C
</abbr>
and
its
Members.
<p>
This
is
a
draft
document
and
may
be
updated,
replaced
or
obsoleted
by
other
documents
at
any
time.
It
is
inappropriate
to
cite
this
document
as
other
than
work
in
progress.
<p>
This
document
was
produced
by
a
group
operating
under
the
<a href="https://www.w3.org/Consortium/Patent-Policy/">
<abbr title="World Wide Web Consortium">
W3C
</abbr>

</a>.
<abbr title="World Wide Web Consortium">

</abbr>

</a>
made
in
connection
with
the

of

An

who

knowledge
of

<a href="https://www.w3.org/Consortium/Patent-Policy/#def-essential">
Essential
Claim(s)
</a>
must

</a>.

<section class=informative id=introduction>
<div class=header-wrapper>
<h2 id=x1-introduction>
<bdi class=secno>
1.
</bdi>
Introduction
</h2>
<a class=self-link href=#introduction aria-label="Permalink for Section 1.">
</a>
</div>
<p>
<em>
This
section
is
non-normative.
</em>
</p>
<p>
Accurately
measuring
performance
characteristics
of
web
applications
is
an
important
aspect
of
making
web
applications
faster.
This
specification
defines
the
necessary
<a href=#dfn-performance-timeline class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-performance-timeline-1>
Performance
Timeline
</a>
primitives
that
enable
web
developers
to
access,
instrument,
and
retrieve
various
performance
metrics
from
the
full
lifecycle
of
a
web
application.
</p>
<p>
[
<cite>
<a class=bibref data-link-type=biblio href=#bib-navigation-timing-2 title="Navigation Timing Level 2">
NAVIGATION-TIMING-2
</a>
</cite>
],
[
<cite>
<a class=bibref data-link-type=biblio href=#bib-resource-timing-2 title="Resource Timing Level 2">
RESOURCE-TIMING-2
</a>
</cite>
],
and
[
<cite>
<a class=bibref data-link-type=biblio href=#bib-user-timing-2 title="User Timing Level 2">
USER-TIMING-2
</a>
</cite>
]
are
examples
of
specifications
that
define
timing
information
related
to
the
navigation
of
the
document,
resources
on
the
page,
and
developer
scripts,
respectively.
Together
these
and
other
performance
interfaces
define
performance
metrics
that
describe
the
<a href=#dfn-performance-timeline class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-performance-timeline-2>
Performance
Timeline
</a>
of
a
web
application.
For
example,
the
following
script
shows
how
a
developer
can
access
the
<a href=#dfn-performance-timeline class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-performance-timeline-3>
Performance
Timeline
</a>
to
obtain
performance
metrics
related
to
the
navigation
of
the
document,
resources
on
the
page,
and
developer
scripts:
</p>
<div class=example id=example-1>
<div class=marker>
<a class=self-link href=#example-1>
Example
<bdi>
1
</bdi>
</a>
  </div> <pre aria-busy=false><code class="hljs html"><span class=hljs-meta>&lt;!doctype <span class=hljs-meta-keyword>html</span>&gt;</span>
<span class=hljs-tag>&lt;<span class=hljs-name>html</span>&gt;</span>
<span class=hljs-tag>&lt;<span class=hljs-name>head</span>&gt;</span><span class=hljs-tag>&lt;/<span class=hljs-name>head</span>&gt;</span>
<span class=hljs-tag>&lt;<span class=hljs-name>body</span> <span class=hljs-attr>onload</span>=<span class=hljs-string>"init()"</span>&gt;</span>
  <span class=hljs-tag>&lt;<span class=hljs-name>img</span> <span class=hljs-attr>id</span>=<span class=hljs-string>"image0"</span> <span class=hljs-attr>src</span>=<span class=hljs-string>"https://www.w3.org/Icons/w3c_main.png"</span> /&gt;</span>
  <span class=hljs-tag>&lt;<span class=hljs-name>script</span>&gt;</span><span class=javascript>
    <span class=hljs-function><span class=hljs-keyword>function</span> <span class=hljs-title>init</span>(<span class=hljs-params></span>) </span>{
      <span class=hljs-comment>// see [[USER-TIMING-2]]</span>
      performance.mark(<span class=hljs-string>"startWork"</span>);
      doWork(); <span class=hljs-comment>// Some developer code</span>
      performance.mark(<span class=hljs-string>"endWork"</span>);
      measurePerf();
    }
    <span class=hljs-function><span class=hljs-keyword>function</span> <span class=hljs-title>measurePerf</span>(<span class=hljs-params></span>) </span>{
      performance
        .getEntries()
        .map(<span class=hljs-function><span class=hljs-params>entry</span> =&gt;</span> <span class=hljs-built_in>JSON</span>.stringify(entry, <span class=hljs-literal>null</span>, <span class=hljs-number>2</span>))
        .forEach(<span class=hljs-function><span class=hljs-params>json</span> =&gt;</span> <span class=hljs-built_in>console</span>.log(json));
    }
  </span><span class=hljs-tag>&lt;/<span class=hljs-name>script</span>&gt;</span>
  <span class=hljs-tag>&lt;/<span class=hljs-name>body</span>&gt;</span>
<span class=hljs-tag>
&lt;/
<span class=hljs-name>
html
</span>
&gt;
</span>
</code>
</pre>
</div>
<p>
Alternatively,
the
developer
can
observe
the
<a href=#dfn-performance-timeline class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-performance-timeline-4>
Performance
Timeline
</a>
and
be
notified
of
new
performance
metrics
and,
optionally,
previously
buffered
performance
metrics
of
specified
type,
via
the
<a href=#dom-performanceobserver class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserver-2>
<code>
PerformanceObserver
</code>
</a>
interface.
</p>
<p>
The
<a href=#dom-performanceobserver class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserver-3>
<code>
PerformanceObserver
</code>
</a>
interface
was
added
<del class="diff-old">in
Performance
Timeline
Level
2
</del>
and
is
designed
to
address
limitations
of
the
buffer-based
approach
shown
in
the
first
example.
By
using
the
PerformanceObserver
interface,
the
application
can:
</p>
<ul>
<li>
Avoid
polling
the
timeline
to
detect
new
metrics
</li>
<li>
Eliminate
costly
deduplication
logic
to
identify
new
metrics
</li>
<li>
Eliminate
race
conditions
with
other
consumers
that
may
want
to
manipulate
the
buffer
</li>
</ul>
<p>
The
developer
is
encouraged
to
use
<a href=#dom-performanceobserver class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserver-4>
<code>
PerformanceObserver
</code>
</a>
where
possible.
Further,
new
performance
API's
and
metrics
may
only
be
available
through
the
<a href=#dom-performanceobserver class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserver-5>
<code>
PerformanceObserver
</code>
</a>
interface.
The
observer
works
by
specifying
a
callback
in
the
constructor
and
specifying
the
performance
entries
it's
interested
in
via
the
<a href=#dom-performanceobserver-observe class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserver-observe-1>
<code>
observe()
</code>
</a>
method.
The
user
agent
chooses
when
to
execute
the
callback,
which
receives
performance
entries
that
have
been
queued.
</p>
<p>
There
are
special
considerations
regarding
initial
page
load
when
using
the
<a href=#dom-performanceobserver class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserver-6>
<code>
PerformanceObserver
</code>
</a>
interface:
a
registration
must
be
active
to
receive
events
but
the
registration
script
may
not
be
available
or
may
not
be
desired
in
the
critical
path.
To
address
this,
user
agents
buffer
some
number
of
events
while
the
page
is
being
constructed,
and
these
buffered
events
can
be
accessed
via
the
<a href=#dom-performanceobserverinit-buffered class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserverinit-buffered-1>
<code>
buffered
</code>
</a>
flag
when
registering
the
observer.
When
this
flag
is
set,
the
user
agent
retrieves
and
dispatches
events
that
it
has
buffered,
for
the
specified
entry
type,
and
delivers
them
in
the
first
callback
after
the
<a href=#dom-performanceobserver-observe class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserver-observe-2>
<code>
observe()
</code>
</a>
call
occurs.
</p>
<div class=note role=note id=issue-container-generatedID>
<div role=heading class="note-title marker" id=h-note aria-level=3>
<span>
Note
</span>
</div>
<p class="">
The
number
of
buffered
events
is
determined
by
the
specification
that
defines
the
metric
and
buffering
is
intended
to
used
for
first-N
events
only;
buffering
is
not
unbounded
or
continuous.
</div>
<div class=example id=example-2>
<div class=marker>
<a class=self-link href=#example-2>
Example
<bdi>
2
</bdi>
</a>
  </div> <pre aria-busy=false><code class="hljs xml"><span class=hljs-meta>&lt;!doctype <span class=hljs-meta-keyword>html</span>&gt;</span>
<span class=hljs-tag>&lt;<span class=hljs-name>html</span>&gt;</span>
<span class=hljs-tag>&lt;<span class=hljs-name>head</span>&gt;</span><span class=hljs-tag>&lt;/<span class=hljs-name>head</span>&gt;</span>
<span class=hljs-tag>&lt;<span class=hljs-name>body</span>&gt;</span>
<span class=hljs-tag>&lt;<span class=hljs-name>img</span> <span class=hljs-attr>id</span>=<span class=hljs-string>"image0"</span> <span class=hljs-attr>src</span>=<span class=hljs-string>"https://www.w3.org/Icons/w3c_main.png"</span> /&gt;</span>
<span class=hljs-tag>&lt;<span class=hljs-name>script</span>&gt;</span><span class=javascript>
<span class=hljs-comment>// Know when the entry types we would like to use are not supported.</span>
<span class=hljs-function><span class=hljs-keyword>function</span> <span class=hljs-title>detectSupport</span>(<span class=hljs-params>entryTypes</span>) </span>{
  <span class=hljs-keyword>for</span> (<span class=hljs-keyword>const</span> entryType <span class=hljs-keyword>of</span> entryTypes) {
    <span class=hljs-keyword>if</span> (!PerformanceObserver.supportedEntryTypes.includes(entryType)) {
      <span class=hljs-comment>// Indicate to client-side analytics that |entryType| is not supported.</span>
    }
  }
}
detectSupport([<span class=hljs-string>"resource"</span>, <span class=hljs-string>"mark"</span>, <span class=hljs-string>"measure"</span>]);
<span class=hljs-keyword>const</span> userTimingObserver = <span class=hljs-keyword>new</span> PerformanceObserver(<span class=hljs-function><span class=hljs-params>list</span> =&gt;</span> {
  list
    .getEntries()
    <span class=hljs-comment>// Get the values we are interested in</span>
    .map(<span class=hljs-function>(<span class=hljs-params>{ name, entryType, startTime, duration }</span>) =&gt;</span> {
      <span class=hljs-keyword>const</span> obj = {
        <span class=hljs-string>"Duration"</span>: duration,
        <span class=hljs-string>"Entry Type"</span>: entryType,
        <span class=hljs-string>"Name"</span>: name,
        <span class=hljs-string>"Start Time"</span>: startTime,
      };
      <span class=hljs-keyword>return</span> <span class=hljs-built_in>JSON</span>.stringify(obj, <span class=hljs-literal>null</span>, <span class=hljs-number>2</span>);
    })
    <span class=hljs-comment>// Display them to the console.</span>
    .forEach(<span class=hljs-built_in>console</span>.log);
  <span class=hljs-comment>// Disconnect after processing the events.</span>
  userTimingObserver.disconnect();
});
<span class=hljs-comment>// Subscribe to new events for User-Timing.</span>
userTimingObserver.observe({<span class=hljs-attr>entryTypes</span>: [<span class=hljs-string>"mark"</span>, <span class=hljs-string>"measure"</span>]});

    <span class=hljs-comment>// Get the values we are interested in</span>

<section id=conformance>
<div class=header-wrapper>
<h2 id=x2-conformance>
<bdi class=secno>
2.
</bdi>
Conformance
</h2>
<a class=self-link href=#conformance aria-label="Permalink for Section 2.">
</a>
</div>
<p>
As
well
as
sections
marked
as
non-normative,
all
authoring
guidelines,
diagrams,
examples,
and
notes
in
this
specification
are
non-normative.
Everything
else
in
this
specification
is
normative.
<p>
The
key
words
<em class=rfc2119>
MUST
</em>,
<em class=rfc2119>
MUST
NOT
</em>,
and
<em class=rfc2119>
SHOULD
</em>
in
this
document
are
to
be
interpreted
as
described
in
<a href="https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/bcp14">
BCP
14
</a>
[
<cite>
<a class=bibref data-link-type=biblio href=#bib-rfc2119 title="Key words for use in RFCs to Indicate Requirement Levels">
RFC2119
</a>
</cite>
]
[
<cite>
<a class=bibref data-link-type=biblio href=#bib-rfc8174 title="Ambiguity of Uppercase vs Lowercase in RFC 2119 Key Words">
RFC8174
</a>
</cite>
]
when,
and
only
when,
they
appear
in
all
capitals,
as
shown
here.
</p>
<p>
Conformance
requirements
phrased
as
algorithms
or
specific
steps
may
be
implemented
in
any
manner,
so
long
as
the
end
result
is
equivalent.
(In
particular,
the
algorithms
defined
in
this
specification
are
intended
to
be
easy
to
follow,
and
not
intended
to
be
performant).
</p>
</section>
<section id=performance-timeline>
<div class=header-wrapper>
<h2 id=x3-performance-timeline>
<bdi class=secno>
3.
</bdi>
<dfn id=dfn-performance-timeline tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog data-dfn-type=dfn>
Performance
Timeline
</dfn>
</h2>
<a class=self-link href=#performance-timeline aria-label="Permalink for Section 3.">
</a>
</div>
<p>
Each
<a data-type=dfn href="https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object">
global
object
</a>
has:
</p>
<ul>
<li>
a
<dfn id=dfn-performance-observer-task-queued-flag tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog data-dfn-type=dfn>
performance
observer
task
queued
flag
</dfn>
</li>
<li>
a
<dfn id=dfn-list-of-registered-performance-observer-objects tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog data-dfn-type=dfn>
list
of
<a href=#dfn-registered-performance-observer class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-registered-performance-observer-1>
registered
performance
observer
</a>
objects
</dfn>
that
is
initially
empty
</li>
<li>
a
<dfn id=dfn-performance-entry-buffer-map tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog data-dfn-type=dfn>
performance
entry
buffer
map
</dfn>
<a data-type=dfn href="https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ordered-map">
map
</a>,
<a data-link-type=dfn href="https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-key">
keyed
</a>
on
a
<code>
DOMString
</code>,
representing
the
entry
type
to
which
the
buffer
belongs.
The
<a data-type=dfn href="https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ordered-map">
map
</a>
's
<a href="https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-value">
value
</a>
is
the
following
tuple:
<ul>
<li>
A
<dfn class=export data-export="" id=dfn-performance-entry-buffer tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog data-dfn-type=dfn>
performance
entry
buffer
</dfn>
to
store
<a href=#dom-performanceentry class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceentry-2>
<code>
PerformanceEntry
</code>
</a>
objects,
that
is
initially
empty.
</li>
<li>
An
integer
<dfn id=dfn-maxbuffersize tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog data-dfn-type=dfn>
maxBufferSize
</dfn>,
initialized
to
the
<a href="https://w3c.github.io/timing-entrytypes-registry/#registry">
registry
</a>
value
for
this
entry
type.
</li>
<li>
A
<code>
boolean
</code>
<dfn id=dfn-availablefromtimeline tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog data-dfn-type=dfn>
availableFromTimeline
</dfn>,
initialized
to
the
<a href="https://w3c.github.io/timing-entrytypes-registry/#registry">
registry
</a>
value
for
this
entry
type.
</li>
<li>
An
integer
<dfn id=dfn-dropped-entries-count tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog data-dfn-type=dfn>
dropped
entries
count
</dfn>
that
is
initially
0.
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p>
In
order
to
get
the
<dfn id=dfn-relevant-performance-entry-tuple tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog data-dfn-type=dfn>
relevant
performance
entry
tuple
</dfn>,
given
<var>
entryType
</var>
and
<var>
globalObject
</var>
as
input,
run
the
following
steps:
</p>
<ol>
<li>
Let
<var>
map
</var>
be
the
<a href=#dfn-performance-entry-buffer-map class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-performance-entry-buffer-map-1>
performance
entry
buffer
map
</a>
associated
with
<var>
globalObject
</var>.
</li>
<li>
Return
the
result
of
<a data-link-type=dfn href="https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-get">
getting
the
value
of
an
entry
</a>
from
<var>
map
</var>,
given
<var>
entryType
</var>
as
the
<a data-link-type=dfn href="https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-key">
key
</a>.
</li>
</ol>
<aside class=mdn>
<details>
<summary aria-label="Expand MDN details for Performance">
<span>
MDN
</span>
<span title="This feature is in all major engines.">
?
</span>
</summary>
<a title="The Performance interface provides access to performance-related information for the current page. It's part of the High Resolution Time API, but is enhanced by the Performance Timeline API, the Navigation Timing API, the User Timing API, and the Resource Timing API." href="https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/Performance">
Performance
</a>
<p class=engines-all>
This
feature
is
in
all
major
engines.
</p>

</td>

<section data-dfn-for=Performance data-link-for=Performance id=extensions-to-the-performance-interface>
<div class=header-wrapper>
<h3 id=x3-1-extensions-to-the-performance-interface>
<bdi class=secno>
3.1
</bdi>
Extensions
to
the
<a data-link-type=idl data-lt=Performance data-type=interface href="https://www.w3.org/TR/hr-time-3/#dom-performance">
<code>
Performance
</code>
</a>
interface
</h3>
<a class=self-link href=#extensions-to-the-performance-interface aria-label="Permalink for Section 3.1">
</a>
</div>
<p>
This
extends
the
<a data-link-type=idl data-lt=Performance data-type=interface href="https://www.w3.org/TR/hr-time-3/#dom-performance">
<code>
Performance
</code>
</a>
interface
from
[
<cite>
<a class=bibref data-link-type=biblio href=#bib-hr-time-3 title="High Resolution Time">
HR-TIME-3
</a>
</cite>
]
and
hosts
performance
related
attributes
and
methods
used
to
retrieve
the
performance
metric
data
from
the
<a href=#dfn-performance-timeline class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-performance-timeline-5>
Performance
Timeline
</a>.
</p>
      <pre class="idl def" id=webidl-1930846793><span class=idlHeader><a class=self-link href=#webidl-1930846793>WebIDL</a></span><code><span data-idl="" class=idlInterface id=idl-def-performance-partial-1 data-title=Performance>partial interface <a data-idl=partial data-link-type=interface data-title=Performance class=idlID data-dfn-for=Performance data-type=interface href="https://www.w3.org/TR/hr-time-3/#dom-performance">Performance</a> {<span data-idl="" class=idlMethod id=idl-def-performance-getentries data-title=getEntries data-dfn-for=Performance><span class=idlType>

<section id=getentriesbytype-method>
<div class=header-wrapper>
<h4 id=x3-1-2-getentriesbytype-method>
<bdi class=secno>

<section data-dfn-for=PerformanceEntry data-link-for=PerformanceEntry id=the-performanceentry-interface>
<div class=header-wrapper>
<h2 id=x4-the-performanceentry-interface>
<bdi class=secno>
4.
</bdi>
The
<dfn data-export="" data-dfn-type=interface id=dom-performanceentry data-idl=interface data-title=PerformanceEntry data-dfn-for="" tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog>
<code>
PerformanceEntry
</code>
</dfn>
interface
</h2>
<a class=self-link href=#the-performanceentry-interface aria-label="Permalink for Section 4.">
</a>
</div>
<p>
The
<a href=#dom-performanceentry class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceentry-5>
<code>
PerformanceEntry
</code>
</a>
interface
hosts
the
performance
data
of
various
metrics.
</p>
    <pre class="idl def" id=webidl-1748587153><span class=idlHeader><a class=self-link href=#webidl-1748587153>WebIDL</a></span><code><span data-idl="" class=idlInterface id=idl-def-performanceentry data-title=PerformanceEntry>[<span class=extAttr><a data-type=extended-attribute href="https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#Exposed">Exposed</a>=(Window,Worker)</span>]
interface <a class="internalDFN idlID" data-link-type=interface href=#dom-performanceentry id=ref-for-dom-performanceentry-6><code>PerformanceEntry</code></a> {<span data-idl="" class=idlAttribute id=idl-def-performanceentry-name data-title=name data-dfn-for=PerformanceEntry>
  readonly    attribute<span class=idlType> <a data-type=interface href="https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMString">DOMString</a></span>           <a class="internalDFN idlName" data-link-type=attribute href=#dom-performanceentry-name id=ref-for-dom-performanceentry-name-1><code>name</code></a>;</span><span data-idl="" class=idlAttribute id=idl-def-performanceentry-entrytype data-title=entryType data-dfn-for=PerformanceEntry>
  readonly    attribute<span class=idlType> <a data-type=interface href="https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMString">DOMString</a></span>           <a class="internalDFN idlName" data-link-type=attribute href=#dom-performanceentry-entrytype id=ref-for-dom-performanceentry-entrytype-1><code>entryType</code></a>;</span><span data-idl="" class=idlAttribute id=idl-def-performanceentry-starttime data-title=startTime data-dfn-for=PerformanceEntry>
  readonly    attribute<span class=idlType> <a data-type=typedef href="https://www.w3.org/TR/hr-time-3/#dom-domhighrestimestamp">DOMHighResTimeStamp</a></span> <a class="internalDFN idlName" data-link-type=attribute href=#dom-performanceentry-starttime id=ref-for-dom-performanceentry-starttime-1><code>startTime</code></a>;</span><span data-idl="" class=idlAttribute id=idl-def-performanceentry-duration data-title=duration data-dfn-for=PerformanceEntry>
  readonly    attribute<span class=idlType> <a data-type=typedef href="https://www.w3.org/TR/hr-time-3/#dom-domhighrestimestamp">DOMHighResTimeStamp</a></span> <a class="internalDFN idlName" data-link-type=attribute href=#dom-performanceentry-duration id=ref-for-dom-performanceentry-duration-1><code>duration</code></a>;</span><span data-idl="" class=idlMethod id=idl-def-performanceentry-tojson data-title=toJSON data-dfn-for=PerformanceEntry>
  [<span class=extAttr><a data-type=extended-attribute href="https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#Default">Default</a></span>]<span class=idlType> <a data-type=interface href="https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-object">object</a></span> <a class="internalDFN idlName" data-link-type=method href=#dom-performanceentry-tojson id=ref-for-dom-performanceentry-tojson-1><code>toJSON</code></a>();</span>
};
</span>
</code>
</pre>
<dl>
<dt>
<dfn data-export="" data-dfn-type=attribute id=dom-performanceentry-name data-idl=attribute data-title=name data-dfn-for=PerformanceEntry data-type=DOMString data-lt=name data-local-lt=PerformanceEntry.name tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog>
<code>
name
</code>
</dfn>
</dt>
<dd>
This
attribute
<em class=rfc2119>
MUST
</em>
return
an
identifier
for
this
<a href=#dom-performanceentry class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceentry-7>
<code>
PerformanceEntry
</code>
</a>
object.
This
identifier
does
not
have
to
be
unique.
</dd>
<dt>
<dfn data-export="" data-dfn-type=attribute id=dom-performanceentry-entrytype data-idl=attribute data-title=entryType data-dfn-for=PerformanceEntry data-type=DOMString data-lt=entryType data-local-lt=PerformanceEntry.entryType tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog>
<code>
entryType
</code>
</dfn>
</dt>
<dd>
This
attribute
<em class=rfc2119>
MUST
</em>
return
the
type
of
the
interface
represented
by
this
<a href=#dom-performanceentry class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceentry-8>
<code>
PerformanceEntry
</code>
</a>
object.
<div class=note role=note id=issue-container-generatedID-0>
<div role=heading class="note-title marker" id=h-note-0 aria-level=3>
<span>
Note
</span>
</div>
<p class="">
All
<code>
entryType
</code>
values
are
defined
in
the
relevant
<a href="https://w3c.github.io/timing-entrytypes-registry/#registry">
registry
</a>.
Examples
include:
<code>
"mark"
</code>
and
<code>
"measure"
</code>
[
<cite>
<a class=bibref data-link-type=biblio href=#bib-user-timing-2 title="User Timing Level 2">
USER-TIMING-2
</a>
</cite>
],
<code>
"navigation"
</code>
[
<cite>
<a class=bibref data-link-type=biblio href=#bib-navigation-timing-2 title="Navigation Timing Level 2">
NAVIGATION-TIMING-2
</a>
</cite>
],
<code>
"resource"
</code>
[
<cite>
<a class=bibref data-link-type=biblio href=#bib-resource-timing-2 title="Resource Timing Level 2">
RESOURCE-TIMING-2
</a>
</cite>
],
and
<code>
"longtask"
</code>.
</div>
</dd>
<dt>
<dfn data-export="" data-dfn-type=attribute id=dom-performanceentry-starttime data-idl=attribute data-title=startTime data-dfn-for=PerformanceEntry data-type=DOMHighResTimeStamp data-lt=startTime data-local-lt=PerformanceEntry.startTime tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog>
<code>
startTime
</code>
</dfn>
</dt>
<dd>
This
attribute
<em class=rfc2119>
MUST
</em>
return
the
time
value
of
the
first
recorded
timestamp
of
this
performance
metric.
If
the
startTime
concept
doesn't
apply,
a
performance
metric
may
choose
to
return
a
<code>
startTime
</code>
of
<code>
0
</code>.
</dd>
<dt>
<dfn data-export="" data-dfn-type=attribute id=dom-performanceentry-duration data-idl=attribute data-title=duration data-dfn-for=PerformanceEntry data-type=DOMHighResTimeStamp data-lt=duration data-local-lt=PerformanceEntry.duration tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog>
<code>
duration
</code>
</dfn>
</dt>
<dd>
This
attribute
<em class=rfc2119>
MUST
</em>
return
the
time
value
of
the
duration
of
the
entire
event
being
recorded
by
this
<a href=#dom-performanceentry class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceentry-9>
<code>
PerformanceEntry
</code>
</a>.
Typically,
this
would
be
the
time
difference
between
the
last
recorded
timestamp
and
the
first
recorded
timestamp
of
this
<a href=#dom-performanceentry class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceentry-10>
<code>
PerformanceEntry
</code>
</a>.
If
the
duration
concept
doesn't
apply,
a
performance
metric
may
choose
to
return
a
<code>
duration
</code>
of
<code>
0
</code>.
</dd>
</dl>
<p>
When
<dfn data-export="" data-dfn-type=method id=dom-performanceentry-tojson data-idl=operation data-title=toJSON data-dfn-for=PerformanceEntry data-type=object data-lt=toJSON() data-local-lt=PerformanceEntry.toJSON|PerformanceEntry.toJSON()|toJSON tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog>
<code>
toJSON
</code>
</dfn>
is
called,
run
[
<cite>
<a class=bibref data-link-type=biblio href=#bib-webidl title="Web IDL Standard">
WebIDL
</a>
</cite>
]'s
<a data-type=dfn href="https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#default-tojson-steps">
default
toJSON
steps
</a>.
</p>
</section>

<section data-link-for=PerformanceObserver data-dfn-for=PerformanceObserver id=the-performanceobserver-interface>
<div class=header-wrapper>
<h2 id=x5-the-performanceobserver-interface>
<bdi class=secno>
5.
</bdi>
The
<dfn data-export="" data-dfn-type=interface id=dom-performanceobserver data-idl=interface data-title=PerformanceObserver data-dfn-for="" tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog>
<code>
PerformanceObserver
</code>
</dfn>
interface
</h2>
<a class=self-link href=#the-performanceobserver-interface aria-label="Permalink for Section 5.">
</a>
</div>
<p>
The
<a href=#dom-performanceobserver class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserver-7>
<code>
PerformanceObserver
</code>
</a>
interface
can
be
used
to
observe
the
<a href=#dfn-performance-timeline class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-performance-timeline-6>
Performance
Timeline
</a>
to
be
notified
of
new
performance
metrics
as
they
are
recorded,
and
optionally
buffered
performance
metrics.
</p>
<p>
Each
<a href=#dom-performanceobserver class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserver-8>
<code>
PerformanceObserver
</code>
</a>
has
these
associated
concepts:
</p>
<ul>
<li>
A
<dfn data-export="" data-dfn-type=callback id=dom-performanceobservercallback data-idl=callback data-title=PerformanceObserverCallback data-dfn-for="" tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog>
<code>
PerformanceObserverCallback
</code>
</dfn>
<dfn id=dfn-observer-callback tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog data-dfn-type=dfn>
observer
callback
</dfn>
set
on
creation.
</li>
<li>
A
<a href=#dom-performanceentrylist class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceentrylist-8>
<code>
PerformanceEntryList
</code>
</a>
object
called
the
<dfn id=dfn-observer-buffer tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog data-dfn-type=dfn>
observer
buffer
</dfn>
that
is
initially
empty.
</li>
<li>
A
<code>
DOMString
</code>
<dfn id=dfn-observer-type tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog data-dfn-type=dfn>
observer
type
</dfn>
which
is
initially
<code>
"undefined"
</code>.
</li>
<li>
A
boolean
<dfn id=dfn-requires-dropped-entries tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog data-dfn-type=dfn>
requires
dropped
entries
</dfn>
which
is
initially
set
to
false.
</li>
</ul>
<p>
The
<code>
PerformanceObserver(callback)
</code>
constructor
must
create
a
new
<a href=#dom-performanceobserver class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserver-9>
<code>
PerformanceObserver
</code>
</a>
object
with
its
<a href=#dfn-observer-callback class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-observer-callback-1>
observer
callback
</a>
set
to
<var>
callback
</var>
and
then
return
it.
</p>
<p>
A
<dfn id=dfn-registered-performance-observer tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog data-dfn-type=dfn>
registered
performance
observer
</dfn>
is
a
<a data-type=dfn href="https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct">
struct
</a>
consisting
of
an
<dfn id=dfn-observer tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog data-dfn-type=dfn>
observer
</dfn>
member
(a
<a href=#dom-performanceobserver class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserver-10>
<code>
PerformanceObserver
</code>
</a>
object)
and
an
<dfn id=dfn-options-list tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog data-dfn-type=dfn>
options
list
</dfn>
member
(a
list
of
<a href=#dom-performanceobserverinit class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserverinit-1>
<code>
PerformanceObserverInit
</code>
</a>
dictionaries).
</p>
    <pre class="idl def" id=webidl-1552896364><span class=idlHeader><a class=self-link href=#webidl-1552896364>WebIDL</a></span><code><span data-idl="" class=idlCallback id=idl-def-performanceobservercallback data-title=PerformanceObserverCallback>callback <a class="internalDFN idlID" data-link-type=callback href=#dom-performanceobservercallback id=ref-for-dom-performanceobservercallback-1><code>PerformanceObserverCallback</code></a> =<span class=idlType> <a data-type=interface href="https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-undefined">undefined</a></span> (<span class=idlType><a href=#dom-performanceobserverentrylist class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserverentrylist-1><code>PerformanceObserverEntryList</code></a></span> <span class=idlParamName>entries</span>,<span class=idlType>
                                             <a href=#dom-performanceobserver class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserver-11><code>PerformanceObserver</code></a></span> <span class=idlParamName>observer</span>,
                                             optional<span class=idlType> <a href=#dom-performanceobservercallbackoptions class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobservercallbackoptions-1><code>PerformanceObserverCallbackOptions</code></a></span> <span class=idlParamName>options</span> = {});</span><span data-idl="" class=idlInterface id=idl-def-performanceobserver data-title=PerformanceObserver>
[<span class=extAttr><a data-type=extended-attribute href="https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#Exposed">Exposed</a>=(Window,Worker)</span>]
interface <a class="internalDFN idlID" data-link-type=interface href=#dom-performanceobserver id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserver-12><code>PerformanceObserver</code></a> {<span data-idl="" class=idlConstructor id=idl-def-performanceobserver-constructor-callback data-title=constructor data-dfn-for=PerformanceObserver>
  <dfn data-export="" data-dfn-type=constructor id=dom-performanceobserver-constructor data-idl=constructor data-title=constructor data-dfn-for=PerformanceObserver data-lt=constructor()|constructor(callback) data-local-lt=PerformanceObserver.constructor|PerformanceObserver.constructor()|constructor tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog><code>constructor</code></dfn>(<span class=idlType><a href=#dom-performanceobservercallback class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobservercallback-2><code>PerformanceObserverCallback</code></a></span> <span class=idlParamName>callback</span>);</span><span data-idl="" class=idlMethod id=idl-def-performanceobserver-observe-options data-title=observe data-dfn-for=PerformanceObserver><span class=idlType>
  <a data-type=interface href="https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-undefined">undefined</a></span> <a class="internalDFN idlName" data-link-type=method href=#dom-performanceobserver-observe id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserver-observe-3><code>observe</code></a> (optional<span class=idlType> <a href=#dom-performanceobserverinit class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserverinit-2><code>PerformanceObserverInit</code></a></span> <span class=idlParamName>options</span> = {});</span><span data-idl="" class=idlMethod id=idl-def-performanceobserver-disconnect data-title=disconnect data-dfn-for=PerformanceObserver><span class=idlType>
  <a data-type=interface href="https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-undefined">undefined</a></span> <a class="internalDFN idlName" data-link-type=method href=#dom-performanceobserver-disconnect id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserver-disconnect-1><code>disconnect</code></a> ();</span><span data-idl="" class=idlMethod id=idl-def-performanceobserver-takerecords data-title=takeRecords data-dfn-for=PerformanceObserver><span class=idlType>
  <a href=#dom-performanceentrylist class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceentrylist-9><code>PerformanceEntryList</code></a></span> <a class="internalDFN idlName" data-link-type=method href=#dom-performanceobserver-takerecords id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserver-takerecords-1><code>takeRecords</code></a>();</span><span data-idl="" class=idlAttribute id=idl-def-performanceobserver-supportedentrytypes data-title=supportedEntryTypes data-dfn-for=PerformanceObserver>
  [<span class=extAttr><a data-type=extended-attribute href="https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#SameObject">SameObject</a></span>] static readonly attribute<span class=idlType> <a data-type=interface href="https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-frozen-array">FrozenArray</a>&lt;<a data-type=interface href="https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMString">DOMString</a>&gt;</span> <a class="internalDFN idlName" data-link-type=attribute href=#dom-performanceobserver-supportedentrytypes id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserver-supportedentrytypes-1><code>supportedEntryTypes</code></a>;</span>
};
</span>
</code>
</pre>
<div class=note role=note id=issue-container-generatedID-1>
<div role=heading class="note-title marker" id=h-note-1 aria-level=3>
<span>
Note
</span>
</div>
<p class="">
To
keep
the
performance
overhead
to
minimum
the
application
ought
to
only
subscribe
to
event
types
that
it
is
interested
in,
and
disconnect
the
observer
once
it
no
longer
needs
to
observe
the
performance
data.
Filtering
by
name
is
not
supported,
as
it
would
implicitly
require
a
subscription
for
all
event
types
—
this
is
possible,
but
discouraged,
as
it
will
generate
a
significant
volume
of
events.
</div>
<section data-dfn-for=PerformanceObserverCallbackOptions data-link-for=PerformanceObserverCallbackOptions id=performanceobservercallbackoptions-dictionary>
<div class=header-wrapper>
<h3 id=x5-1-performanceobservercallbackoptions-dictionary>

</code>
</dfn>
dictionary
</h3>
<a class=self-link href=#performanceobservercallbackoptions-dictionary aria-label="Permalink for Section 5.1">

      <pre class="idl def" id=webidl-565716801><span class=idlHeader><a class=self-link href=#webidl-565716801>WebIDL</a></span><code><span data-idl="" class=idlDictionary id=idl-def-performanceobservercallbackoptions data-title=PerformanceObserverCallbackOptions>dictionary <a class="internalDFN idlID" data-link-type=dictionary href=#dom-performanceobservercallbackoptions id=ref-for-dom-performanceobservercallbackoptions-2><code>PerformanceObserverCallbackOptions</code></a> {<span data-idl="" class=idlMember id=idl-def-performanceobservercallbackoptions-droppedentriescount data-title=droppedEntriesCount data-dfn-for=PerformanceObserverCallbackOptions><span class=idlType>

set.

<section id=observe-method>
<div class=header-wrapper>
<h3 id=x5-2-observe-method>
<bdi class=secno>
5.2
</bdi>
<dfn data-export="" data-dfn-type=method id=dom-performanceobserver-observe data-idl=operation data-title=observe() data-dfn-for=PerformanceObserver data-type=undefined data-lt=observe()|observe(options) data-local-lt=PerformanceObserver.observe|PerformanceObserver.observe()|observe tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog>
<code>
observe()
</code>
</dfn>
method
</h3>
<a class=self-link href=#observe-method aria-label="Permalink for Section 5.2">
</a>
</div>
<p>
The
<a href=#dom-performanceobserver-observe class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserver-observe-4>
<code>
observe()
</code>
</a>
method
instructs
the
user
agent
to
register
the
observer
and
must
run
these
steps:
</p>
<ol data-link-for=PerformanceObserverInit>
<li>
Let
<var>
relevantGlobal
</var>
be
<a data-type=dfn href="https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this">
this
</a>
's
<a data-type=dfn href="https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-global">
relevant
global
object
</a>.
</li>
<li>
If
<var>
options
</var>
's
<a href=#dom-performanceobserverinit-entrytypes class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserverinit-entrytypes-1>
<code>
entryTypes
</code>
</a>
and
<a href=#dom-performanceobserverinit-type class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserverinit-type-1>
<code>
type
</code>
</a>
members
are
both
omitted,
then
<a data-link-type=dfn data-type=dfn href="https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw">
throw
</a>
a
"
<a data-link-type=idl data-type=exception href="https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror">
<code>
TypeError
</code>
</a>
".
</li>
<li>
If
<var>
options
</var>
's
<a href=#dom-performanceobserverinit-entrytypes class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserverinit-entrytypes-2>
<code>
entryTypes
</code>
</a>
is
present
and
any
other
member
is
also
present,
then
<a data-link-type=dfn data-type=dfn href="https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw">
throw
</a>
a
"
<a data-link-type=idl data-type=exception href="https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#exceptiondef-typeerror">
<code>
TypeError
</code>
</a>
".
</li>
<li>
Update
or
check
<a data-type=dfn href="https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this">
this
</a>
's
<a href=#dfn-observer-type class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-observer-type-1>
observer
type
</a>
by
running
these
steps:
<ol>
<li>
If

<a data-type=dfn href="https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this">

<li>

<li>

<div class=note role=note id=issue-container-generatedID-2>
<div role=heading class="note-title marker" id=h-note-2 aria-level=4>
<span>
Note
</span>
</div>
<p class="">
A
<a href=#dom-performanceobserver class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserver-14>
<code>
PerformanceObserver
</code>
</a>
object
needs
to
always
call
<a href=#dom-performanceobserver-observe class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserver-observe-5>
<code>
observe()
</code>
</a>
with
<var>
options
</var>
's
<a href=#dom-performanceobserverinit-entrytypes class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserverinit-entrytypes-6>
<code>
entryTypes
</code>
</a>
set
OR
always
call
<a href=#dom-performanceobserver-observe class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserver-observe-6>
<code>
observe()
</code>
</a>
with
<var>
options
</var>
's
<a href=#dom-performanceobserverinit-type class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserverinit-type-8>
<code>
type
</code>
</a>
set.
If
one
<a href=#dom-performanceobserver class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserver-15>
<code>
PerformanceObserver
</code>
</a>
calls
<a href=#dom-performanceobserver-observe class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserver-observe-7>
<code>
observe()
</code>
</a>
with
<a href=#dom-performanceobserverinit-entrytypes class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserverinit-entrytypes-7>
<code>
entryTypes
</code>
</a>
and
also
calls
observe
with
<a href=#dom-performanceobserverinit-type class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserverinit-type-9>
<code>
type
</code>
</a>,
then
an
exception
is
thrown.
This
is
meant
to
avoid
confusion
with
how
calls
would
stack.
When
using
<a href=#dom-performanceobserverinit-entrytypes class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserverinit-entrytypes-8>
<code>
entryTypes
</code>
</a>,
no
other
parameters
in
<a href=#dom-performanceobserverinit class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserverinit-4>
<code>
PerformanceObserverInit
</code>
</a>
can
be
used.
In
addition,
multiple
<a href=#dom-performanceobserver-observe class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserver-observe-8>
<code>
observe()
</code>
</a>
calls
will
override
for
backwards
compatibility
and
because
a
single
call
should
suffice
in
this
case.
On
the
other
hand,
when
using
<a href=#dom-performanceobserverinit-type class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserverinit-type-10>
<code>
type
</code>
</a>,
calls
will
stack
because
a
single
call
can
only
specify
one
type.
Calling
<a href=#dom-performanceobserver-observe class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserver-observe-9>
<code>
observe()
</code>
</a>
with
a
repeated
<a href=#dom-performanceobserverinit-type class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserverinit-type-11>
<code>
type
</code>
</a>
will
also
override.
</div>

<dl>
<dt>
<dfn data-export="" data-dfn-type=dict-member id=dom-performanceobserverinit-entrytypes data-idl=field data-title=entryTypes data-dfn-for=PerformanceObserverInit data-type=sequence tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog>
<code>
entryTypes
</code>
</dfn>
</dt>
<dd>
A
list
of
entry
types
to
be
observed.
If
present,
the
list
<em class=rfc2119>
MUST
NOT
</em>
be
empty
and
all
other
members
<em class=rfc2119>
MUST
NOT
</em>
be
present.
Types
not
recognized
by
the
user
agent
<em class=rfc2119>
MUST
</em>
be
ignored.
</dd>
</dl>
<dl>
<dt>
<dfn data-export="" data-dfn-type=dict-member id=dom-performanceobserverinit-type data-idl=field data-title=type data-dfn-for=PerformanceObserverInit data-type=DOMString tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog>
<code>
type
</code>
</dfn>
</dt>
<dd>
A
single
entry
type
to
be
observed.
A
type
that
is
not
recognized
by
the
user
agent
<em class=rfc2119>
MUST
</em>
be
ignored.
Other
members
may
be
present.
</dd>
</dl>
<dl>
<dt>
<dfn data-export="" data-dfn-type=dict-member id=dom-performanceobserverinit-buffered data-idl=field data-title=buffered data-dfn-for=PerformanceObserverInit data-type=boolean tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog>
<code>
buffered
</code>
</dfn>
</dt>
<dd>
A
flag
to
indicate
whether
buffered
entries
should
be
queued
into
observer's
buffer.
</dd>
</dl>

PerformanceObserverEntryList
</a>
<p class=engines-all>
This
feature
is
in
all
major
engines.
</p>

<td>
Chrome
</td>
<td>
52+
</td>
<tr class="chrome_android unknown">
<td>
Chrome
Android
</td>
<td>
?
</td>
<tr class="edge unknown">
<td>
Edge
</td>

<dfn data-export="" data-dfn-type=method id=dom-performanceobserver-disconnect data-idl=operation data-title=disconnect() data-dfn-for=PerformanceObserver data-type=undefined data-lt=disconnect() data-local-lt=PerformanceObserver.disconnect|PerformanceObserver.disconnect()|disconnect tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog>

disconnect()
</code>
</dfn>
method
</h3>
<a class=self-link href=#disconnect-method aria-label="Permalink for Section 5.4">
</a>
</div>
<p>
The
<a href=#dom-performanceobserver-disconnect class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserver-disconnect-2>
<code>
disconnect()
</code>
</a>
method
must
do
the
following:
</p>
<ol>
<li>
Remove
<a data-type=dfn href="https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this">
this
</a>
from
the
<a href=#dfn-list-of-registered-performance-observer-objects class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-list-of-registered-performance-observer-objects-5>
list
of
registered
performance
observer
objects
</a>
of
<a data-type=dfn href="https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-global">
relevant
global
object
</a>.
</li>
<li>
Empty
<a data-type=dfn href="https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this">
this
</a>
's
<a href=#dfn-observer-buffer class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-observer-buffer-4>
observer
buffer
</a>.
</li>
<li>
Empty
<a data-type=dfn href="https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this">
this
</a>
's
<a href=#dfn-options-list class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-options-list-7>
options
list
</a>.
</li>
</ol>
</section>

<section id=supportedentrytypes-attribute>
<div class=header-wrapper>
<h3 id=x5-5-supportedentrytypes-attribute>
<bdi class=secno>
5.5
</bdi>
<a href=#dom-performanceobserver-supportedentrytypes class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserver-supportedentrytypes-2>
<code>
supportedEntryTypes
</code>
</a>
attribute
</h3>
<a class=self-link href=#supportedentrytypes-attribute aria-label="Permalink for Section 5.5">
</a>
</div>
<p>
Each
<a href="https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object">
global
object
</a>
has
an
associated
<dfn id=dfn-frozen-array-of-supported-entry-types tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog data-dfn-type=dfn>
frozen
array
of
supported
entry
types
</dfn>,
which
is
initialized
to
the
<a href="https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#es-frozen-array">
FrozenArray
</a>
<a href="https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-create-frozen-array">
created
</a>
from
the
sequence
of
strings
among
the
<a href="https://w3c.github.io/timing-entrytypes-registry/#registry">
registry
</a>
that
are
supported
for
the
global
object,
in
alphabetical
order.
</p>
<p>
When
<dfn data-export="" data-dfn-type=attribute id=dom-performanceobserver-supportedentrytypes data-idl=attribute data-title=supportedEntryTypes data-dfn-for=PerformanceObserver data-type=FrozenArray data-lt=supportedEntryTypes data-local-lt=PerformanceObserver.supportedEntryTypes tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog>
<code>
supportedEntryTypes
</code>
</dfn>
's
attribute
getter
is
called,
run
the
following
steps:
</p>
<ol>
<li>
Let
<var>
globalObject
</var>
be
the
<a href="https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-settings-object-global">
environment
settings
object's
global
object
</a>.
</li>
<li>
Return
<var>
globalObject
</var>
's
<a href=#dfn-frozen-array-of-supported-entry-types class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-frozen-array-of-supported-entry-types-3>
frozen
array
of
supported
entry
types
</a>.
</li>
</ol>
<div class=note role=note id=issue-container-generatedID-3>
<div role=heading class="note-title marker" id=h-note-3 aria-level=4>
<span>
Note
</span>
</div>
<p class="">
This
attribute
allows
web
developers
to
easily
know
which
entry
types
are
supported
by
the
user
agent.
</div>
</section>
</section>
<section id=processing>
<div class=header-wrapper>

</bdi>

<section data-link-for=PerformanceObserver id=queue-a-performanceentry>
<div class=header-wrapper>
<h3 id=x6-1-queue-a-performanceentry>
<bdi class=secno>
6.1
</bdi>
Queue
a
<code>
PerformanceEntry
</code>
</h3>
<a class=self-link href=#queue-a-performanceentry aria-label="Permalink for Section 6.1">
</a>
</div>
<p>
To
<dfn class=export data-export="" id=dfn-queue-a-performanceentry tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog data-dfn-type=dfn>
queue
a
PerformanceEntry
</dfn>
(
<var>
newEntry
</var>
),
run
these
steps:
</p>
<ol>
<li>
Let
<var>
interested
observers
</var>
be
an
initially
empty
set
of
<a href=#dom-performanceobserver class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserver-16>
<code>
PerformanceObserver
</code>
</a>
objects.
</li>
<li>
Let
<var>
entryType
</var>
be
<var>
newEntry
</var>
’s
<a data-lt=PerformanceEntry.entryType href=#dom-performanceentry-entrytype class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceentry-entrytype-2>
<code>
entryType
</code>
</a>
value.
</li>
<li>
Let
<var>
relevantGlobal
</var>
be
<var>
newEntry
</var>
's
<a data-type=dfn href="https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-global">
relevant
global
object
</a>.
</li>
<li>
For
each
<a href=#dfn-registered-performance-observer class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-registered-performance-observer-6>
registered
performance
observer
</a>
<var>
regObs
</var>
in
<var>
relevantGlobal
</var>
's
<a href=#dfn-list-of-registered-performance-observer-objects class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-list-of-registered-performance-observer-objects-6>
list
of
registered
performance
observer
objects
</a>:
<ol>
<li>
<p>
If
<var>
regObs
</var>
's
<a href=#dfn-options-list class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-options-list-8>
options
list
</a>
contains
a
<a href=#dom-performanceobserverinit class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserverinit-6>
<code>
PerformanceObserverInit
</code>
</a>
<var>
options
</var>
whose
<a href=#dom-performanceobserverinit-entrytypes class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserverinit-entrytypes-10>
<code>
entryTypes
</code>
</a>
member
includes
<var>
entryType
</var>
or
whose
<a href=#dom-performanceobserverinit-type class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserverinit-type-13>
<code>
type
</code>
</a>
member
equals
to
<var>
entryType
</var>:
</p>
<ol>
<li>
If
<a href="https://w3c.github.io/timing-entrytypes-registry/#dfn-should-add-entry">
should
add
entry
</a>
with
<var>
newEntry
</var>
and
<var>
options
</var>
returns
true,
append
<var>
regObs
</var>
's
<a href=#dfn-observer class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-observer-5>
observer
</a>
to
<var>
interested
observers
</var>.
</li>
</ol>
</li>
</ol>
</li>
<li>
For
each
<var>
observer
</var>
in
<var>
interested
observers
</var>:
<ol>
<li>
Append
<var>
newEntry
</var>
to
<var>
observer
</var>
's
<a href=#dfn-observer-buffer class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-observer-buffer-5>
observer
buffer
</a>.
</li>
</ol>
</li>
<li>
Let
<var>
tuple
</var>
be
the
<a href=#dfn-relevant-performance-entry-tuple class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-relevant-performance-entry-tuple-2>
relevant
performance
entry
tuple
</a>
of
<var>
entryType
</var>
and
<var>
relevantGlobal
</var>.
</li>
<li>
Let
<var>
isBufferFull
</var>
be
the
return
value
of
the
<a href=#dfn-determine-if-a-performance-entry-buffer-is-full class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-determine-if-a-performance-entry-buffer-is-full-1>
determine
if
a
performance
entry
buffer
is
full
</a>
algorithm
with
<var>
tuple
</var>
as
input.
</li>
<li>
Let
<var>
shouldAdd
</var>
be
the
result
of
<a href="https://w3c.github.io/timing-entrytypes-registry/#dfn-should-add-entry">
should
add
entry
</a>
with
<var>
newEntry
</var>
as
input.
</li>
<li>
If
<var>
isBufferFull
</var>
is
false
and
<var>
shouldAdd
</var>
is
true,
<a data-link-type=dfn data-type=dfn href="https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-append">
append
</a>
<var>
newEntry
</var>
to
<var>
tuple
</var>
's
<a href=#dfn-performance-entry-buffer class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-performance-entry-buffer-2>
performance
entry
buffer
</a>.
</li>
<li>
<a href=#dfn-queue-the-performanceobserver-task class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-queue-the-performanceobserver-task-2>
Queue
the
PerformanceObserver
task
</a>
with
<var>
relevantGlobal
</var>
as
input.
</li>
</ol>
</section>
<section data-link-for=PerformanceObserver id=queue-the-performanceobserver-task>
<div class=header-wrapper>
<h3 id=x6-2-queue-the-performanceobserver-task>
<bdi class=secno>
6.2
</bdi>
Queue
the
PerformanceObserver
task
</h3>
<a class=self-link href=#queue-the-performanceobserver-task aria-label="Permalink for Section 6.2">
</a>
</div>
<p>
When
asked
to
<dfn id=dfn-queue-the-performanceobserver-task tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog data-dfn-type=dfn>
queue
the
PerformanceObserver
task
</dfn>,
given
<var>
relevantGlobal
</var>
as
input,
run
the
following
steps:
</p>
<ol>
<li>
If
<var>
relevantGlobal
</var>
's
<a href=#dfn-performance-observer-task-queued-flag class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-performance-observer-task-queued-flag-1>
performance
observer
task
queued
flag
</a>
is
set,
terminate
these
steps.
</li>
<li>
Set
<var>
relevantGlobal
</var>
's
<a href=#dfn-performance-observer-task-queued-flag class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-performance-observer-task-queued-flag-2>
performance
observer
task
queued
flag
</a>.
</li>
<li>
<a data-type=dfn href="https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task">
Queue
a
task
</a>
that
consists
of
running
the
following
substeps.
The
<a data-type=dfn href="https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#task-source">
task
source
</a>
for
the
queued
task
is
the
<dfn class=export data-export="" id=dfn-performance-timeline-task-source tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog data-dfn-type=dfn>
performance
timeline
task
source
</dfn>.
<ol>
<li>
Unset
<a href=#dfn-performance-observer-task-queued-flag class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-performance-observer-task-queued-flag-3>
performance
observer
task
queued
flag
</a>
of
<var>
relevantGlobal
</var>.
</li>
<li>
Let
<var>
notifyList
</var>
be
a
copy
of
<var>
relevantGlobal
</var>
's
<a href=#dfn-list-of-registered-performance-observer-objects class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-list-of-registered-performance-observer-objects-7>
list
of
registered
performance
observer
objects
</a>.
</li>
<li>
For
each
<a href=#dfn-registered-performance-observer class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-registered-performance-observer-7>
registered
performance
observer
</a>
object
<var>
registeredObserver
</var>
in
<var>
notifyList
</var>,
run
these
steps:
<ol>
<li>
Let
<var>
po
</var>
be
<var>
registeredObserver
</var>
's
<a href=#dfn-observer class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-observer-6>
observer
</a>.
</li>
<li>
Let
<var>
entries
</var>
be
a
copy
of
<var>
po
</var>
’s
<a href=#dfn-observer-buffer class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-observer-buffer-6>
observer
buffer
</a>.
</li>
<li>
If
<var>
entries
</var>
is
empty,
return.
</li>
<li>
Empty
<var>
po
</var>
’s
<a href=#dfn-observer-buffer class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-observer-buffer-7>
observer
buffer
</a>.
</li>
<li>
Let
<var>
observerEntryList
</var>
be
a
new
<a data-link-type=idl data-lt=PerformanceObserverEntryList href=#dom-performanceobserverentrylist class=internalDFN id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserverentrylist-4>
<code>
PerformanceObserverEntryList
</code>
</a>,
with
its
<a href=#dfn-entry-list class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-entry-list-4>
entry
list
</a>
set
to
<var>
entries
</var>.
</li>
<li>
Let
<var>
droppedEntriesCount
</var>
be
null.
</li>
<li>
If
<var>
po
</var>
's
<a href=#dfn-requires-dropped-entries class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-requires-dropped-entries-3>
requires
dropped
entries
</a>
is
set,
perform
the
following
steps:
<ol>
<li>
Set
<var>
droppedEntriesCount
</var>
to
0.
</li>
<li>
For
each
<a href=#dom-performanceobserverinit class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserverinit-7>
<code>
PerformanceObserverInit
</code>
</a>
<var>
item
</var>
in
<var>
registeredObserver
</var>
's
<a href=#dfn-options-list class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-options-list-9>
options
list
</a>:
<ol>
<li>
For
each
<a href="https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMString">
DOMString
</a>
<var>
entryType
</var>
that
appears
either
as
<var>
item
</var>
's
<a href=#dom-performanceobserverinit-type class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserverinit-type-14>
<code>
type
</code>
</a>
or
in
<var>
item
</var>
's
<a href=#dom-performanceobserverinit-entrytypes class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobserverinit-entrytypes-11>
<code>
entryTypes
</code>
</a>:
<ol>
<li>
Let
<var>
map
</var>
be
<var>
relevantGlobal
</var>
's
<a href=#dfn-performance-entry-buffer-map class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-performance-entry-buffer-map-2>
performance
entry
buffer
map
</a>.
</li>
<li>
Let
<var>
tuple
</var>
be
the
result
of
<a data-link-type=dfn href="https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-get">
getting
the
value
of
entry
</a>
on
<var>
map
</var>
given
<var>
entryType
</var>
as
<a data-link-type=dfn href="https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-key">
key
</a>.
</li>
<li>
Increase
<var>
droppedEntriesCount
</var>
by
<var>
tuple
</var>
's
<a href=#dfn-dropped-entries-count class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-dropped-entries-count-1>
dropped
entries
count
</a>.
</li>
</ol>
</li>
</ol>
</li>
<li>
Set
<var>
po
</var>
's
<a href=#dfn-requires-dropped-entries class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-requires-dropped-entries-4>
requires
dropped
entries
</a>
to
false.
</li>
</ol>
</li>
<li>
Let
<var>
callbackOptions
</var>
be
a
<a href=#dom-performanceobservercallbackoptions class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobservercallbackoptions-3>
<code>
PerformanceObserverCallbackOptions
</code>
</a>
with
its
<a href=#dom-performanceobservercallbackoptions-droppedentriescount class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceobservercallbackoptions-droppedentriescount-2>
<code>
droppedEntriesCount
</code>
</a>
set
to
<var>
droppedEntriesCount
</var>
if
<var>
droppedEntriesCount
</var>
is
not
null,
otherwise
unset.
</li>
<li>
Call
<var>
po
</var>
’s
<a href=#dfn-observer-callback class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-observer-callback-2>
observer
callback
</a>
with
<var>
observerEntryList
</var>
as
the
first
argument,
with
<var>
po
</var>
as
the
second
argument
and
as
<a data-type=dfn href="https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-callback-this-value">
callback
this
value
</a>,
and
with
<var>
callbackOptions
</var>
as
the
third
argument.
If
this
<a data-link-type=dfn data-type=dfn href="https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#dfn-throw">
throws
</a>
an
exception,
<a data-type=dfn href="https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#report-the-exception">
report
the
exception
</a>.
</li>
</ol>
</li>
</ol>
</li>
</ol>
<p>
The
<i>
performance
timeline
</i>
<a data-lt="queue a task" data-type=dfn href="https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-task">
task
queue
</a>
is
a
low
priority
queue
that,
if
possible,
should
be
processed
by
the
user
agent
during
idle
periods
to
minimize
impact
of
performance
monitoring
code.
</p>
</section>
<section data-link-for=PerformanceEntry id=filter-buffer-map-by-name-and-type>
<div class=header-wrapper>
<h3 id=x6-3-filter-buffer-map-by-name-and-type>
<bdi class=secno>
6.3
</bdi>
Filter
buffer
map
by
name
and
type
</h3>
<a class=self-link href=#filter-buffer-map-by-name-and-type aria-label="Permalink for Section 6.3">
</a>
</div>
<p>
When
asked
to
run
the
<dfn id=dfn-filter-buffer-map-by-name-and-type tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog data-dfn-type=dfn>
filter
buffer
map
by
name
and
type
</dfn>
algorithm
with
optional
<var>
name
</var>
and
<var>
type
</var>,
run
the
following
steps:
</p>
<ol>
<li>
Let
<var>
result
</var>
be
an
initially
empty
<a data-type=dfn href="https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list">
list
</a>.
</li>
<li>
Let
<var>
map
</var>
be
the
<a href=#dfn-performance-entry-buffer-map class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-performance-entry-buffer-map-3>
performance
entry
buffer
map
</a>
associated
with
the
<a data-type=dfn href="https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#concept-relevant-global">
relevant
global
object
</a>
of
<a data-type=dfn href="https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this">
this
</a>.
</li>
<li>
Let
<var>
tuple
list
</var>
be
an
empty
<a data-type=dfn href="https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list">
list
</a>.
</li>
<li>
If
<var>
type
</var>
is
not
null,
append
the
result
of
<a data-link-type=dfn href="https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-get">
getting
the
value
of
entry
</a>
on
<var>
map
</var>
given
<var>
type
</var>
as
<a data-link-type=dfn href="https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-key">
key
</a>
to
<var>
tuple
list
</var>.
Otherwise,
assign
the
result
of
<a href="https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-getting-the-values">
get
the
values
</a>
on
<var>
map
</var>
to
<var>
tuple
list
</var>.
</li>
<li>
For
each
<var>
tuple
</var>
in
<var>
tuple
list
</var>,
run
the
following
steps:
<ol>
<li>
Let
<var>
buffer
</var>
be
<var>
tuple
</var>
's
<a href=#dfn-performance-entry-buffer class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-performance-entry-buffer-3>
performance
entry
buffer
</a>.
</li>
<li>
If
<var>
tuple
</var>
's
<a href=#dfn-availablefromtimeline class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-availablefromtimeline-1>
availableFromTimeline
</a>
is
false,
continue
to
the
next
<var>
tuple
</var>.
</li>
<li>
Let
<var>
entries
</var>
be
the
result
of
running
<a href=#dfn-filter-buffer-by-name-and-type class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-filter-buffer-by-name-and-type-4>
filter
buffer
by
name
and
type
</a>
with
<var>
buffer
</var>,
<var>
name
</var>
and
<var>
type
</var>
as
inputs.
</li>
<li>
For
each
<var>
entry
</var>
in
<var>
entries
</var>,
<a data-link-type=dfn data-type=dfn href="https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-append">
append
</a>
<var>
entry
</var>
to
<var>
result
</var>.
</li>
</ol>
</li>
<li>
Sort
<var>
results
</var>
's
entries
in
chronological
order
with
respect
to
<a href=#dom-performanceentry-starttime class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceentry-starttime-2>
<code>
startTime
</code>
</a>
</li>
<li>
Return
<var>
result
</var>.
</li>
</ol>
</section>
<section data-link-for=PerformanceEntry id=filter-buffer-by-name-and-type>
<div class=header-wrapper>
<h3 id=x6-4-filter-buffer-by-name-and-type>
<bdi class=secno>
6.4
</bdi>
Filter
buffer
by
name
and
type
</h3>
<a class=self-link href=#filter-buffer-by-name-and-type aria-label="Permalink for Section 6.4">
</a>
</div>
<p>
When
asked
to
run
the
<dfn id=dfn-filter-buffer-by-name-and-type tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog data-dfn-type=dfn>
filter
buffer
by
name
and
type
</dfn>
algorithm,
with
<var>
buffer
</var>,
<var>
name
</var>,
and
<var>
type
</var>
as
inputs,
run
the
following
steps:
</p>
<ol>
<li>
Let
<var>
result
</var>
be
an
initially
empty
<a data-type=dfn href="https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list">
list
</a>.
</li>
<li>
For
each
<a href=#dom-performanceentry class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceentry-11>
<code>
PerformanceEntry
</code>
</a>
<var>
entry
</var>
in
<var>
buffer
</var>,
run
the
following
steps:
<ol>
<li>
If
<var>
type
</var>
is
not
null
and
if
<var>
type
</var>
is
not
<a href="https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#string-is">
identical
to
</a>
<var>
entry
</var>
's
<code>
entryType
</code>
attribute,
continue
to
next
<var>
entry
</var>.
</li>
<li>
If
<var>
name
</var>
is
not
null
and
if
<var>
name
</var>
is
not
<a href="https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#string-is">
identical
to
</a>
<var>
entry
</var>
's
<code>
name
</code>
attribute,
continue
to
next
<var>
entry
</var>.
</li>
<li>
<a data-link-type=dfn data-type=dfn href="https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-append">
append
</a>
<var>
entry
</var>
to
<var>
result
</var>.
</li>
</ol>
</li>
<li>
Sort
<var>
results
</var>
's
entries
in
chronological
order
with
respect
to
<a href=#dom-performanceentry-starttime class=internalDFN data-link-type=idl id=ref-for-dom-performanceentry-starttime-3>
<code>
startTime
</code>
</a>
</li>
<li>
Return
<var>
result
</var>.
</li>
</ol>
</section>
<section data-link-for=PerformanceObserver id=determine-if-a-performance-entry-buffer-is-full>
<div class=header-wrapper>
<h3 id=x6-5-determine-if-a-performance-entry-buffer-is-full>
<bdi class=secno>
6.5
</bdi>
Determine
if
a
performance
entry
buffer
is
full
</h3>
<a class=self-link href=#determine-if-a-performance-entry-buffer-is-full aria-label="Permalink for Section 6.5">
</a>
</div>
<p>
To
<dfn id=dfn-determine-if-a-performance-entry-buffer-is-full tabindex=0 aria-haspopup=dialog data-dfn-type=dfn>
determine
if
a
performance
entry
buffer
is
full
</dfn>,
with
<var>
tuple
</var>
as
input,
run
the
following
steps:
</p>
<ol>
<li>
Let
<var>
num
current
entries
</var>
be
the
size
of
<var>
tuple
</var>
's
<a href=#dfn-performance-entry-buffer class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-performance-entry-buffer-4>
performance
entry
buffer
</a>.
</li>
<li>
If
<var>
num
current
entries
</var>
is
less
than
<var>
tuples
</var>
's
<a href=#dfn-maxbuffersize class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-maxbuffersize-1>
maxBufferSize
</a>,
return
false.
</li>
<li>
Increase
<var>
tuple
</var>
's
<a href=#dfn-dropped-entries-count class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-dropped-entries-count-2>
dropped
entries
count
</a>
by
1.
</li>
<li>
Return
true.
</li>
</ol>
</section>
</section>
<section id=privacy>
<div class=header-wrapper>
<h2 id=x7-privacy-considerations>
<bdi class=secno>
7.
</bdi>
Privacy
Considerations
</h2>
<a class=self-link href=#privacy aria-label="Permalink for Section 7.">
</a>
</div>
<p>
This
specification
extends
the
<a data-link-type=idl data-lt=Performance data-type=interface href="https://www.w3.org/TR/hr-time-3/#dom-performance">
<code>
Performance
</code>
</a>
interface
defined
by
[
<cite>
<a class=bibref data-link-type=biblio href=#bib-hr-time-3 title="High Resolution Time">
HR-TIME-3
</a>
</cite>
]
and
provides
methods
to
queue
and
retrieve
entries
from
the
<a href=#dfn-performance-timeline class=internalDFN data-link-type=dfn id=ref-for-dfn-performance-timeline-7>
performance
timeline
</a>.
Please
refer
to
[
<cite>
<a class=bibref data-link-type=biblio href=#bib-hr-time-3 title="High Resolution Time">
HR-TIME-3
</a>
</cite>
]
for
privacy
considerations
of
exposing
high-resoluting
timing
information.
Each
new
specification
introducing
new
performance
entries
should
have
its
own
privacy
considerations
as
well.
</p>
</section>

<script id=respec-dfn-panel>
(()
=>
{
//
@ts-check
if
(document.respec)
{
document.respec.ready.then(setupPanel);
}
else
{
setupPanel();
}
function
setupPanel()
{
const
listener
=
panelListener();
document.body.addEventListener("keydown",
listener);
document.body.addEventListener("click",
listener);
}
function
panelListener()
{
/**
@type
{HTMLElement}
*/
let
panel
=
null;
return
event
=>
{
const
{
target,
type
}
=
event;
if
(!(target
instanceof
HTMLElement))
return;
//
For
keys,
we
only
care
about
Enter
key
to
activate
the
panel
//
otherwise
it's
activated
via
a
click.
if
(type
===
"keydown"
&&
event.key
!==
"Enter")
return;
const
action
=
deriveAction(event);
switch
(action)
{
case
"show":
{
hidePanel(panel);
/**
@type
{HTMLElement}
*/
const
dfn
=
target.closest("dfn,
.index-term");
panel
=
document.getElementById(`dfn-panel-for-${dfn.id}`);
const
coords
=
deriveCoordinates(event);
displayPanel(dfn,
panel,
coords);
break;
}
case
"dock":
{
panel.style.left
=
null;
panel.style.top
=
null;
panel.classList.add("docked");
break;
}
case
"hide":
{
hidePanel(panel);
panel
=
null;
break;
}
}
};
}
/**
*
@param
{MouseEvent|KeyboardEvent}
event
*/
function
deriveCoordinates(event)
{
const
target
=
/**
@type
HTMLElement
*/
(event.target);
//
We
prevent
synthetic
AT
clicks
from
putting
//
the
dialog
in
a
weird
place.
The
AT
events
sometimes
//
lack
coordinates,
so
they
have
clientX/Y
=
0
const
rect
=
target.getBoundingClientRect();
if
(
event
instanceof
MouseEvent
&&
event.clientX
>
=
rect.left
&&
event.clientY
>
=
rect.top
)
{
//
The
event
probably
happened
inside
the
bounding
rect...
return
{
x:
event.clientX,
y:
event.clientY
};
}
//
Offset
to
the
middle
of
the
element
const
x
=
rect.x
+
rect.width
/
2;
//
Placed
at
the
bottom
of
the
element
const
y
=
rect.y
+
rect.height;
return
{
x,
y
};
}
/**
*
@param
{Event}
event
*/
function
deriveAction(event)
{
const
target
=
/**
@type
{HTMLElement}
*/
(event.target);
const
hitALink
=
!!target.closest("a");
if
(target.closest("dfn:not([data-cite]),
.index-term"))
{
return
hitALink
?
"none"
:
"show";
}
if
(target.closest(".dfn-panel"))
{
if
(hitALink)
{
return
target.classList.contains("self-link")
?
"hide"
:
"dock";
}
const
panel
=
target.closest(".dfn-panel");
return
panel.classList.contains("docked")
?
"hide"
:
"none";
}
if
(document.querySelector(".dfn-panel:not([hidden])"))
{
return
"hide";
}
return
"none";
}
/**
*
@param
{HTMLElement}
dfn
*
@param
{HTMLElement}
panel
*
@param
{{
x:
number,
y:
number
}}
clickPosition
*/
function
displayPanel(dfn,
panel,
{
x,
y
})
{
panel.hidden
=
false;
//
distance
(px)
between
edge
of
panel
and
the
pointing
triangle
(caret)
const
MARGIN
=
20;
const
dfnRects
=
dfn.getClientRects();
//
Find
the
`top`
offset
when
the
`dfn`
can
be
spread
across
multiple
lines
let
closestTop
=
0;
let
minDiff
=
Infinity;
for
(const
rect
of
dfnRects)
{
const
{
top,
bottom
}
=
rect;
const
diffFromClickY
=
Math.abs((top
+
bottom)
/
2
-
y);
if
(diffFromClickY
< minDiff) { minDiff = diffFromClickY; closestTop = top; } } const top = window.scrollY + closestTop + dfnRects[0].height; const left = x - MARGIN; panel.style.left = `${left}px`; panel.style.top = `${top}px`; // Find if the panel is flowing out of the window const panelRect = panel.getBoundingClientRect(); const SCREEN_WIDTH = Math.min(window.innerWidth, window.screen.width); if (panelRect.right >
SCREEN_WIDTH)
{
const
newLeft
=
Math.max(MARGIN,
x
+
MARGIN
-
panelRect.width);
const
newCaretOffset
=
left
-
newLeft;
panel.style.left
=
`${newLeft}px`;
/**
@type
{HTMLElement}
*/
const
caret
=
panel.querySelector(".caret");
caret.style.left
=
`${newCaretOffset}px`;
}
//
As
it's
a
dialog,
we
trap
focus.
//
TODO:
when
<dialog>
becomes
a
implemented,
we
should
really
//
use
that.
trapFocus(panel,
dfn);
}
/**
*
@param
{HTMLElement}
panel
*
@param
{HTMLElement}
dfn
*
@returns
*/
function
trapFocus(panel,
dfn)
{
/**
@type
NodeListOf
<HTMLAnchorElement>
elements
*/
const
anchors
=
panel.querySelectorAll("a[href]");
//
No
need
to
trap
focus
if
(!anchors.length)
return;
//
Move
focus
to
first
anchor
element
const
first
=
anchors.item(0);
first.focus();
const
trapListener
=
createTrapListener(anchors,
panel,
dfn);
panel.addEventListener("keydown",
trapListener);
//
Hiding
the
panel
releases
the
trap
const
mo
=
new
MutationObserver(records
=>
{
const
[record]
=
records;
const
target
=
/**
@type
HTMLElement
*/
(record.target);
if
(target.hidden)
{
panel.removeEventListener("keydown",
trapListener);
mo.disconnect();
}
});
mo.observe(panel,
{
attributes:
true,
attributeFilter:
["hidden"]
});
}
/**
*
*
@param
{NodeListOf
<HTMLAnchorElement>
}
anchors
*
@param
{HTMLElement}
panel
*
@param
{HTMLElement}
dfn
*
@returns
*/
function
createTrapListener(anchors,
panel,
dfn)
{
const
lastIndex
=
anchors.length
-
1;
let
currentIndex
=
0;
return
event
=>
{
switch
(event.key)
{
//
Hitting
"Tab"
traps
us
in
a
nice
loop
around
elements.
case
"Tab":
{
event.preventDefault();
currentIndex
+=
event.shiftKey
?
-1
:
+1;
if
(currentIndex
< 0) { currentIndex = lastIndex; } else if (currentIndex >
lastIndex)
{
currentIndex
=
0;
}
anchors.item(currentIndex).focus();
break;
}
//
Hitting
"Enter"
on
an
anchor
releases
the
trap.
case
"Enter":
hidePanel(panel);
break;
//
Hitting
"Escape"
returns
focus
to
dfn.
case
"Escape":
hidePanel(panel);
dfn.focus();
return;
}
};
}
/**
@param
{HTMLElement}
panel
*/
function
hidePanel(panel)
{
if
(!panel)
return;
panel.hidden
=
true;
panel.classList.remove("docked");
}
})()
</script>

</script>
